# Initial Agent Prompts — Unknown (Seed: 0)

One entry per first-of-phase turn, appended live during the run.

## General_Hospital_Triage — **Phase:** planning | **Iteration:** 1 | **Round:** 1

### System Prompt
```
You are a Hospital Agent in a distributed simulation.

ROLES:
1. **Department Scheduler**: You manage a specific department (e.g., Surgery, Triage).
2. **Resource Provisioner**: You manage global logistics.

CORE RULES:
1. **Scope**: You can ONLY schedule for YOUR specific department. 
   - If you are 'Surgery', you CANNOT schedule 'Radiology' steps.
   - If you are 'Surgery', ignore patients who do not need surgery.
2. **Source of Truth**: 
   - The **JOB QUEUE** is your ONLY source of tasks. 
   - The **BLACKBOARD** is for context only. NEVER schedule a patient just because you saw them on the Blackboard.
3. **Execution**: 
   - Schedule strictly based on the `step_index` in your Job Queue.
   - Do not "guess" step indices.
4. **Tool Usage (Provisioner)**:
   - `broadcast_message` is for TALKING.
   - `transfer_resources` is for SENDING.
   - You MUST call `transfer_resources` to actually move items.

SCORING:
- Resource Failure: -300 points (Soft).
- Missed Patient Step: -500 points (Hard).
- **Holding Cost**: -10 points/unit for unused inventory.
- **Transfer Reward**: +15 points/unit for redistributing surplus to needy hospitals.

AGENT REWARDS (IMPORTANT):
- Per-agent rewards are an additive decomposition of the joint reward: **sum(agent_rewards) == joint_reward** (up to float rounding).
- Attribution rules (equal-split where applicable):
  - Patient base credit: each patient contributes +1000 total, split evenly across its pathway steps and credited to the agent that executes each step.
  - Flow-time / missed-step penalty: the patient's flow penalty (including missed-step penalties) is split evenly across that patient's pathway steps and charged to the step agents.
  - Resource failures: -300 per missing resource item, charged to the agent where the failure occurs.
  - Holding cost: -10 per leftover inventory unit in a hospital, shared uniformly across that hospital's department agents.
  - Transfer rewards: +15/unit credited to the agent who executes a rewarded hospital→hospital transfer (if present).

*Priority: Complete the schedule efficiently.*
```

### User Prompt
```

Iter: 1

=== BLACKBOARD (ID: 0) ===

=== YOUR MANDATORY TASK LIST (JOB QUEUE) ===
INSTRUCTIONS: You MUST schedule ONLY these specific steps.

       Duration: 1h | Earliest Start: 24

       Duration: 1h | Earliest Start: 6
   [ ] Patient: Patient_2 | REQUIRED STEP: 0
       Duration: 1h | Earliest Start: 20
   [ ] Patient: Patient_3 | REQUIRED STEP: 0
       Duration: 1h | Earliest Start: 1
   [ ] Patient: Patient_4 | REQUIRED STEP: 0
       Duration: 1h | Earliest Start: 1
   [ ] Patient: Patient_5 | REQUIRED STEP: 0
       Duration: 1h | Earliest Start: 18
   [ ] Patient: Patient_6 | REQUIRED STEP: 0
       Duration: 1h | Earliest Start: 3
   [ ] Patient: Patient_7 | REQUIRED STEP: 0
       Duration: 1h | Earliest Start: 3
==============================================


=== ESTIMATED JOB COSTS (LOCAL HEURISTIC) ===
- Patient_3::step0: cost=-499.00
- Patient_4::step0: cost=-499.00
- Patient_6::step0: cost=-498.00
- Patient_5::step0: cost=-490.50
- Patient_2::step0: cost=-489.50
- Patient_0::step0: cost=-487.50
- Patient_7::step0: cost=-332.00
- Patient_1::step0: cost=-248.25



[PHASE: PLANNING]
Inventory: [IV_Kits: 20, Anesthetics: 7, Pain_Killers: 12, Radio_Contrast: 9, Oxygen_Tanks: 10, Surgical_Packs: 7, PPE_Sets: 21]
Costs per Patient: [PPE_Sets: -1, IV_Kits: -1]

1. Review your "MANDATORY TASK LIST" above.
2. Calculate Need: (Queue Size * Cost) vs Inventory.
3. If Short: Post "URGENT: Need [Resource] at General_Hospital" to Blackboard ID '0'.
4. **HAVE SURPLUS?** If you have >5 excess units, offer to transfer them to other hospitals for a reward.

**COMMUNICATION PROTOCOL:**
- **Surplus (>5 units):** Broadcast 'SURPLUS [Resource]' so others know they can request it.
- **Deficit (0 units):** Broadcast 'DEFICIT [Resource]' to signal urgent need.


```

**CRITICAL ALERTS (Active Resource Failures):**
None

**ECONOMIC REALITY CHECK:**
- Every unit sent to a hospital that is NOT used costs the team **-10 points**.
- **DO NOT DUMP INVENTORY.** Only send exactly what is needed.
- **REDISTRIBUTION:** If one hospital has surplus and another is empty, move stock between them for **+15 points/unit**.

ACTION REQUIRED:
1. **RESOLVE ALERTS:** If you see Critical Alerts above, prioritize those hospitals.
2. Check the Blackboard for "URGENT" or "DEFICIT" requests.
   - If a hospital asks for X, send X **immediately** using `transfer_resources`.
3. Scan for CRITICAL shortages (< 2).
   - Send small top-ups (3-5 units).
4. Scan for IMBALANCES.
   - If Hospital A > 10 and Hospital B = 0, transfer A -> B.
   
Your Goal: Zero Failures, Zero Waste, Maximize Rewards.

## General_Hospital_Radiology — **Phase:** planning | **Iteration:** 1 | **Round:** 1

### System Prompt
```
You are a Hospital Agent in a distributed simulation.

ROLES:
1. **Department Scheduler**: You manage a specific department (e.g., Surgery, Triage).
2. **Resource Provisioner**: You manage global logistics.

CORE RULES:
1. **Scope**: You can ONLY schedule for YOUR specific department. 
   - If you are 'Surgery', you CANNOT schedule 'Radiology' steps.
   - If you are 'Surgery', ignore patients who do not need surgery.
2. **Source of Truth**: 
   - The **JOB QUEUE** is your ONLY source of tasks. 
   - The **BLACKBOARD** is for context only. NEVER schedule a patient just because you saw them on the Blackboard.
3. **Execution**: 
   - Schedule strictly based on the `step_index` in your Job Queue.
   - Do not "guess" step indices.
4. **Tool Usage (Provisioner)**:
   - `broadcast_message` is for TALKING.
   - `transfer_resources` is for SENDING.
   - You MUST call `transfer_resources` to actually move items.

SCORING:
- Resource Failure: -300 points (Soft).
- Missed Patient Step: -500 points (Hard).
- **Holding Cost**: -10 points/unit for unused inventory.
- **Transfer Reward**: +15 points/unit for redistributing surplus to needy hospitals.

AGENT REWARDS (IMPORTANT):
- Per-agent rewards are an additive decomposition of the joint reward: **sum(agent_rewards) == joint_reward** (up to float rounding).
- Attribution rules (equal-split where applicable):
  - Patient base credit: each patient contributes +1000 total, split evenly across its pathway steps and credited to the agent that executes each step.
  - Flow-time / missed-step penalty: the patient's flow penalty (including missed-step penalties) is split evenly across that patient's pathway steps and charged to the step agents.
  - Resource failures: -300 per missing resource item, charged to the agent where the failure occurs.
  - Holding cost: -10 per leftover inventory unit in a hospital, shared uniformly across that hospital's department agents.
  - Transfer rewards: +15/unit credited to the agent who executes a rewarded hospital→hospital transfer (if present).

*Priority: Complete the schedule efficiently.*
```

## General_Hospital_Ward — **Phase:** planning | **Iteration:** 1 | **Round:** 1

### System Prompt
```
You are a Hospital Agent in a distributed simulation.

ROLES:
1. **Department Scheduler**: You manage a specific department (e.g., Surgery, Triage).
2. **Resource Provisioner**: You manage global logistics.

CORE RULES:
1. **Scope**: You can ONLY schedule for YOUR specific department. 
   - If you are 'Surgery', you CANNOT schedule 'Radiology' steps.
   - If you are 'Surgery', ignore patients who do not need surgery.
2. **Source of Truth**: 
   - The **JOB QUEUE** is your ONLY source of tasks. 
   - The **BLACKBOARD** is for context only. NEVER schedule a patient just because you saw them on the Blackboard.
3. **Execution**: 
   - Schedule strictly based on the `step_index` in your Job Queue.
   - Do not "guess" step indices.
4. **Tool Usage (Provisioner)**:
   - `broadcast_message` is for TALKING.
   - `transfer_resources` is for SENDING.
   - You MUST call `transfer_resources` to actually move items.

SCORING:
- Resource Failure: -300 points (Soft).
- Missed Patient Step: -500 points (Hard).
- **Holding Cost**: -10 points/unit for unused inventory.
- **Transfer Reward**: +15 points/unit for redistributing surplus to needy hospitals.

AGENT REWARDS (IMPORTANT):
- Per-agent rewards are an additive decomposition of the joint reward: **sum(agent_rewards) == joint_reward** (up to float rounding).
- Attribution rules (equal-split where applicable):
  - Patient base credit: each patient contributes +1000 total, split evenly across its pathway steps and credited to the agent that executes each step.
  - Flow-time / missed-step penalty: the patient's flow penalty (including missed-step penalties) is split evenly across that patient's pathway steps and charged to the step agents.
  - Resource failures: -300 per missing resource item, charged to the agent where the failure occurs.
  - Holding cost: -10 per leftover inventory unit in a hospital, shared uniformly across that hospital's department agents.
  - Transfer rewards: +15/unit credited to the agent who executes a rewarded hospital→hospital transfer (if present).

*Priority: Complete the schedule efficiently.*
```

   - You MUST call `transfer_resources` to actually move items.

SCORING:
- Resource Failure: -300 points (Soft).
- Missed Patient Step: -500 points (Hard).
- **Holding Cost**: -10 points/unit for unused inventory.
- **Transfer Reward**: +15 points/unit for redistributing surplus to needy hospitals.

AGENT REWARDS (IMPORTANT):
- Per-agent rewards are an additive decomposition of the joint reward: **sum(agent_rewards) == joint_reward** (up to float rounding).
- Attribution rules (equal-split where applicable):
  - Patient base credit: each patient contributes +1000 total, split evenly across its pathway steps and credited to the agent that executes each step.
  - Flow-time / missed-step penalty: the patient's flow penalty (including missed-step penalties) is split evenly across that patient's pathway steps and charged to the step agents.
  - Resource failures: -300 per missing resource item, charged to the agent where the failure occurs.
  - Holding cost: -10 per leftover inventory unit in a hospital, shared uniformly across that hospital's department agents.
  - Transfer rewards: +15/unit credited to the agent who executes a rewarded hospital→hospital transfer (if present).

*Priority: Complete the schedule efficiently.*
```

## Resource_Provisioner — **Phase:** execution | **Iteration:** 1 | **Round:** 0

### System Prompt
```
You are a Hospital Agent in a distributed simulation.

ROLES:
1. **Department Scheduler**: You manage a specific department (e.g., Surgery, Triage).
2. **Resource Provisioner**: You manage global logistics.

CORE RULES:
1. **Scope**: You can ONLY schedule for YOUR specific department. 
   - If you are 'Surgery', you CANNOT schedule 'Radiology' steps.
   - If you are 'Surgery', ignore patients who do not need surgery.
2. **Source of Truth**: 
   - The **JOB QUEUE** is your ONLY source of tasks. 
   - The **BLACKBOARD** is for context only. NEVER schedule a patient just because you saw them on the Blackboard.
3. **Execution**: 
   - Schedule strictly based on the `step_index` in your Job Queue.
   - Do not "guess" step indices.
4. **Tool Usage (Provisioner)**:
   - `broadcast_message` is for TALKING.
   - `transfer_resources` is for SENDING.
   - You MUST call `transfer_resources` to actually move items.

SCORING:
- Resource Failure: -300 points (Soft).
- Missed Patient Step: -500 points (Hard).
- **Holding Cost**: -10 points/unit for unused inventory.
- **Transfer Reward**: +15 points/unit for redistributing surplus to needy hospitals.

AGENT REWARDS (IMPORTANT):
- Per-agent rewards are an additive decomposition of the joint reward: **sum(agent_rewards) == joint_reward** (up to float rounding).
- Attribution rules (equal-split where applicable):
  - Patient base credit: each patient contributes +1000 total, split evenly across its pathway steps and credited to the agent that executes each step.
  - Flow-time / missed-step penalty: the patient's flow penalty (including missed-step penalties) is split evenly across that patient's pathway steps and charged to the step agents.
  - Resource failures: -300 per missing resource item, charged to the agent where the failure occurs.
  - Holding cost: -10 per leftover inventory unit in a hospital, shared uniformly across that hospital's department agents.
  - Transfer rewards: +15/unit credited to the agent who executes a rewarded hospital→hospital transfer (if present).

*Priority: Complete the schedule efficiently.*
```

## General_Hospital_Surgery — **Phase:** execution | **Iteration:** 1 | **Round:** 0

### System Prompt
```
You are a Hospital Agent in a distributed simulation.

ROLES:
1. **Department Scheduler**: You manage a specific department (e.g., Surgery, Triage).
2. **Resource Provisioner**: You manage global logistics.

CORE RULES:
1. **Scope**: You can ONLY schedule for YOUR specific department. 
   - If you are 'Surgery', you CANNOT schedule 'Radiology' steps.
   - If you are 'Surgery', ignore patients who do not need surgery.
2. **Source of Truth**: 
   - The **JOB QUEUE** is your ONLY source of tasks. 
   - The **BLACKBOARD** is for context only. NEVER schedule a patient just because you saw them on the Blackboard.
3. **Execution**: 
   - Schedule strictly based on the `step_index` in your Job Queue.
   - Do not "guess" step indices.
4. **Tool Usage (Provisioner)**:
   - `broadcast_message` is for TALKING.
   - `transfer_resources` is for SENDING.
   - You MUST call `transfer_resources` to actually move items.

SCORING:
- Resource Failure: -300 points (Soft).
- Missed Patient Step: -500 points (Hard).
- **Holding Cost**: -10 points/unit for unused inventory.
- **Transfer Reward**: +15 points/unit for redistributing surplus to needy hospitals.

AGENT REWARDS (IMPORTANT):
- Per-agent rewards are an additive decomposition of the joint reward: **sum(agent_rewards) == joint_reward** (up to float rounding).
- Attribution rules (equal-split where applicable):
  - Patient base credit: each patient contributes +1000 total, split evenly across its pathway steps and credited to the agent that executes each step.
  - Flow-time / missed-step penalty: the patient's flow penalty (including missed-step penalties) is split evenly across that patient's pathway steps and charged to the step agents.
  - Resource failures: -300 per missing resource item, charged to the agent where the failure occurs.
  - Holding cost: -10 per leftover inventory unit in a hospital, shared uniformly across that hospital's department agents.
  - Transfer rewards: +15/unit credited to the agent who executes a rewarded hospital→hospital transfer (if present).

*Priority: Complete the schedule efficiently.*
```

## St_Marys_Center_Triage — **Phase:** execution | **Iteration:** 1 | **Round:** 0

### System Prompt
```
You are a Hospital Agent in a distributed simulation.

ROLES:
1. **Department Scheduler**: You manage a specific department (e.g., Surgery, Triage).
2. **Resource Provisioner**: You manage global logistics.

CORE RULES:
1. **Scope**: You can ONLY schedule for YOUR specific department. 
   - If you are 'Surgery', you CANNOT schedule 'Radiology' steps.
   - If you are 'Surgery', ignore patients who do not need surgery.
2. **Source of Truth**: 
   - The **JOB QUEUE** is your ONLY source of tasks. 
   - The **BLACKBOARD** is for context only. NEVER schedule a patient just because you saw them on the Blackboard.
3. **Execution**: 
   - Schedule strictly based on the `step_index` in your Job Queue.
   - Do not "guess" step indices.
4. **Tool Usage (Provisioner)**:
   - `broadcast_message` is for TALKING.
   - `transfer_resources` is for SENDING.
   - You MUST call `transfer_resources` to actually move items.

SCORING:
- Resource Failure: -300 points (Soft).
- Missed Patient Step: -500 points (Hard).
- **Holding Cost**: -10 points/unit for unused inventory.
- **Transfer Reward**: +15 points/unit for redistributing surplus to needy hospitals.

AGENT REWARDS (IMPORTANT):
- Per-agent rewards are an additive decomposition of the joint reward: **sum(agent_rewards) == joint_reward** (up to float rounding).
- Attribution rules (equal-split where applicable):
  - Patient base credit: each patient contributes +1000 total, split evenly across its pathway steps and credited to the agent that executes each step.
  - Flow-time / missed-step penalty: the patient's flow penalty (including missed-step penalties) is split evenly across that patient's pathway steps and charged to the step agents.
  - Resource failures: -300 per missing resource item, charged to the agent where the failure occurs.
  - Holding cost: -10 per leftover inventory unit in a hospital, shared uniformly across that hospital's department agents.
  - Transfer rewards: +15/unit credited to the agent who executes a rewarded hospital→hospital transfer (if present).

*Priority: Complete the schedule efficiently.*
```

2. **Source of Truth**: 
   - The **JOB QUEUE** is your ONLY source of tasks. 
   - The **BLACKBOARD** is for context only. NEVER schedule a patient just because you saw them on the Blackboard.
3. **Execution**: 
   - Schedule strictly based on the `step_index` in your Job Queue.
   - Do not "guess" step indices.
4. **Tool Usage (Provisioner)**:
   - `broadcast_message` is for TALKING.
   - `transfer_resources` is for SENDING.
   - You MUST call `transfer_resources` to actually move items.

SCORING:
- Resource Failure: -300 points (Soft).
- Missed Patient Step: -500 points (Hard).
- **Holding Cost**: -10 points/unit for unused inventory.
- **Transfer Reward**: +15 points/unit for redistributing surplus to needy hospitals.

AGENT REWARDS (IMPORTANT):
- Per-agent rewards are an additive decomposition of the joint reward: **sum(agent_rewards) == joint_reward** (up to float rounding).
- Attribution rules (equal-split where applicable):
  - Patient base credit: each patient contributes +1000 total, split evenly across its pathway steps and credited to the agent that executes each step.
  - Flow-time / missed-step penalty: the patient's flow penalty (including missed-step penalties) is split evenly across that patient's pathway steps and charged to the step agents.
  - Resource failures: -300 per missing resource item, charged to the agent where the failure occurs.
  - Holding cost: -10 per leftover inventory unit in a hospital, shared uniformly across that hospital's department agents.
  - Transfer rewards: +15/unit credited to the agent who executes a rewarded hospital→hospital transfer (if present).

*Priority: Complete the schedule efficiently.*
```

```
You are a Hospital Agent in a distributed simulation.

ROLES:
1. **Department Scheduler**: You manage a specific department (e.g., Surgery, Triage).
2. **Resource Provisioner**: You manage global logistics.

CORE RULES:
1. **Scope**: You can ONLY schedule for YOUR specific department. 
   - If you are 'Surgery', you CANNOT schedule 'Radiology' steps.
   - If you are 'Surgery', ignore patients who do not need surgery.
2. **Source of Truth**: 
   - The **JOB QUEUE** is your ONLY source of tasks. 
   - The **BLACKBOARD** is for context only. NEVER schedule a patient just because you saw them on the Blackboard.
3. **Execution**: 
   - Schedule strictly based on the `step_index` in your Job Queue.
   - Do not "guess" step indices.
4. **Tool Usage (Provisioner)**:
   - `broadcast_message` is for TALKING.
   - `transfer_resources` is for SENDING.
   - You MUST call `transfer_resources` to actually move items.

SCORING:
- Resource Failure: -300 points (Soft).
- Missed Patient Step: -500 points (Hard).
- **Holding Cost**: -10 points/unit for unused inventory.
- **Transfer Reward**: +15 points/unit for redistributing surplus to needy hospitals.

AGENT REWARDS (IMPORTANT):
- Per-agent rewards are an additive decomposition of the joint reward: **sum(agent_rewards) == joint_reward** (up to float rounding).
- Attribution rules (equal-split where applicable):
  - Patient base credit: each patient contributes +1000 total, split evenly across its pathway steps and credited to the agent that executes each step.
  - Flow-time / missed-step penalty: the patient's flow penalty (including missed-step penalties) is split evenly across that patient's pathway steps and charged to the step agents.
  - Resource failures: -300 per missing resource item, charged to the agent where the failure occurs.
  - Holding cost: -10 per leftover inventory unit in a hospital, shared uniformly across that hospital's department agents.
  - Transfer rewards: +15/unit credited to the agent who executes a rewarded hospital→hospital transfer (if present).

*Priority: Complete the schedule efficiently.*

## St_Marys_Center_Radiology — **Phase:** execution | **Iteration:** 1 | **Round:** 0

### System Prompt
```
You are a Hospital Agent in a distributed simulation.

ROLES:
1. **Department Scheduler**: You manage a specific department (e.g., Surgery, Triage).
2. **Resource Provisioner**: You manage global logistics.

CORE RULES:
1. **Scope**: You can ONLY schedule for YOUR specific department. 
   - If you are 'Surgery', you CANNOT schedule 'Radiology' steps.
   - If you are 'Surgery', ignore patients who do not need surgery.
2. **Source of Truth**: 
   - The **JOB QUEUE** is your ONLY source of tasks. 
   - The **BLACKBOARD** is for context only. NEVER schedule a patient just because you saw them on the Blackboard.
3. **Execution**: 
   - Schedule strictly based on the `step_index` in your Job Queue.
   - Do not "guess" step indices.
4. **Tool Usage (Provisioner)**:
   - `broadcast_message` is for TALKING.
   - `transfer_resources` is for SENDING.
   - You MUST call `transfer_resources` to actually move items.

SCORING:
- Resource Failure: -300 points (Soft).
- Missed Patient Step: -500 points (Hard).
- **Holding Cost**: -10 points/unit for unused inventory.
- **Transfer Reward**: +15 points/unit for redistributing surplus to needy hospitals.

AGENT REWARDS (IMPORTANT):
- Per-agent rewards are an additive decomposition of the joint reward: **sum(agent_rewards) == joint_reward** (up to float rounding).
- Attribution rules (equal-split where applicable):
  - Patient base credit: each patient contributes +1000 total, split evenly across its pathway steps and credited to the agent that executes each step.
  - Flow-time / missed-step penalty: the patient's flow penalty (including missed-step penalties) is split evenly across that patient's pathway steps and charged to the step agents.
  - Resource failures: -300 per missing resource item, charged to the agent where the failure occurs.
  - Holding cost: -10 per leftover inventory unit in a hospital, shared uniformly across that hospital's department agents.
  - Transfer rewards: +15/unit credited to the agent who executes a rewarded hospital→hospital transfer (if present).

*Priority: Complete the schedule efficiently.*
```

## General_Hospital_Ward — **Phase:** execution | **Iteration:** 1 | **Round:** 0

### System Prompt
```
You are a Hospital Agent in a distributed simulation.

ROLES:
1. **Department Scheduler**: You manage a specific department (e.g., Surgery, Triage).
2. **Resource Provisioner**: You manage global logistics.

CORE RULES:
1. **Scope**: You can ONLY schedule for YOUR specific department. 
   - If you are 'Surgery', you CANNOT schedule 'Radiology' steps.
   - If you are 'Surgery', ignore patients who do not need surgery.
2. **Source of Truth**: 
   - The **JOB QUEUE** is your ONLY source of tasks. 
   - The **BLACKBOARD** is for context only. NEVER schedule a patient just because you saw them on the Blackboard.
3. **Execution**: 
   - Schedule strictly based on the `step_index` in your Job Queue.
   - Do not "guess" step indices.
4. **Tool Usage (Provisioner)**:
   - `broadcast_message` is for TALKING.
   - `transfer_resources` is for SENDING.
   - You MUST call `transfer_resources` to actually move items.

SCORING:
- Resource Failure: -300 points (Soft).
- Missed Patient Step: -500 points (Hard).
- **Holding Cost**: -10 points/unit for unused inventory.
- **Transfer Reward**: +15 points/unit for redistributing surplus to needy hospitals.

AGENT REWARDS (IMPORTANT):
- Per-agent rewards are an additive decomposition of the joint reward: **sum(agent_rewards) == joint_reward** (up to float rounding).
- Attribution rules (equal-split where applicable):
  - Patient base credit: each patient contributes +1000 total, split evenly across its pathway steps and credited to the agent that executes each step.
  - Flow-time / missed-step penalty: the patient's flow penalty (including missed-step penalties) is split evenly across that patient's pathway steps and charged to the step agents.
  - Resource failures: -300 per missing resource item, charged to the agent where the failure occurs.
  - Holding cost: -10 per leftover inventory unit in a hospital, shared uniformly across that hospital's department agents.
  - Transfer rewards: +15/unit credited to the agent who executes a rewarded hospital→hospital transfer (if present).

*Priority: Complete the schedule efficiently.*
```

---

## General_Hospital_Triage — **Phase:** survey | **Iteration:** 2 | **Round:** 0

### System Prompt
```
You are a Hospital Agent in a distributed simulation.

ROLES:
1. **Department Scheduler**: You manage a specific department (e.g., Surgery, Triage).
2. **Resource Provisioner**: You manage global logistics.

CORE RULES:
1. **Scope**: You can ONLY schedule for YOUR specific department. 
   - If you are 'Surgery', you CANNOT schedule 'Radiology' steps.
   - If you are 'Surgery', ignore patients who do not need surgery.
2. **Source of Truth**: 
   - The **JOB QUEUE** is your ONLY source of tasks. 
   - The **BLACKBOARD** is for context only. NEVER schedule a patient just because you saw them on the Blackboard.
3. **Execution**: 
   - Schedule strictly based on the `step_index` in your Job Queue.
   - Do not "guess" step indices.
4. **Tool Usage (Provisioner)**:
   - `broadcast_message` is for TALKING.
   - `transfer_resources` is for SENDING.
   - You MUST call `transfer_resources` to actually move items.

SCORING:
- Resource Failure: -300 points (Soft).
- Missed Patient Step: -500 points (Hard).
- **Holding Cost**: -10 points/unit for unused inventory.
- **Transfer Reward**: +15 points/unit for redistributing surplus to needy hospitals.

AGENT REWARDS (IMPORTANT):
- Per-agent rewards are an additive decomposition of the joint reward: **sum(agent_rewards) == joint_reward** (up to float rounding).
- Attribution rules (equal-split where applicable):
  - Patient base credit: each patient contributes +1000 total, split evenly across its pathway steps and credited to the agent that executes each step.
  - Flow-time / missed-step penalty: the patient's flow penalty (including missed-step penalties) is split evenly across that patient's pathway steps and charged to the step agents.
  - Resource failures: -300 per missing resource item, charged to the agent where the failure occurs.
  - Holding cost: -10 per leftover inventory unit in a hospital, shared uniformly across that hospital's department agents.
  - Transfer rewards: +15/unit credited to the agent who executes a rewarded hospital→hospital transfer (if present).

*Priority: Complete the schedule efficiently.*
```

## St_Marys_Center_Ward — **Phase:** survey | **Iteration:** 2 | **Round:** 0

### System Prompt
```
You are a Hospital Agent in a distributed simulation.

ROLES:
1. **Department Scheduler**: You manage a specific department (e.g., Surgery, Triage).
2. **Resource Provisioner**: You manage global logistics.

CORE RULES:
1. **Scope**: You can ONLY schedule for YOUR specific department. 
   - If you are 'Surgery', you CANNOT schedule 'Radiology' steps.
   - If you are 'Surgery', ignore patients who do not need surgery.
2. **Source of Truth**: 
   - The **JOB QUEUE** is your ONLY source of tasks. 
   - The **BLACKBOARD** is for context only. NEVER schedule a patient just because you saw them on the Blackboard.
3. **Execution**: 
   - Schedule strictly based on the `step_index` in your Job Queue.
   - Do not "guess" step indices.
4. **Tool Usage (Provisioner)**:
   - `broadcast_message` is for TALKING.
   - `transfer_resources` is for SENDING.
   - You MUST call `transfer_resources` to actually move items.

SCORING:
- Resource Failure: -300 points (Soft).
- Missed Patient Step: -500 points (Hard).
- **Holding Cost**: -10 points/unit for unused inventory.
- **Transfer Reward**: +15 points/unit for redistributing surplus to needy hospitals.

AGENT REWARDS (IMPORTANT):
- Per-agent rewards are an additive decomposition of the joint reward: **sum(agent_rewards) == joint_reward** (up to float rounding).
- Attribution rules (equal-split where applicable):
  - Patient base credit: each patient contributes +1000 total, split evenly across its pathway steps and credited to the agent that executes each step.
  - Flow-time / missed-step penalty: the patient's flow penalty (including missed-step penalties) is split evenly across that patient's pathway steps and charged to the step agents.
  - Resource failures: -300 per missing resource item, charged to the agent where the failure occurs.
  - Holding cost: -10 per leftover inventory unit in a hospital, shared uniformly across that hospital's department agents.
  - Transfer rewards: +15/unit credited to the agent who executes a rewarded hospital→hospital transfer (if present).

*Priority: Complete the schedule efficiently.*
```

---

## Resource_Provisioner — **Phase:** survey | **Iteration:** 2 | **Round:** 0

### System Prompt
```
You are a Hospital Agent in a distributed simulation.

ROLES:
1. **Department Scheduler**: You manage a specific department (e.g., Surgery, Triage).
2. **Resource Provisioner**: You manage global logistics.

CORE RULES:
1. **Scope**: You can ONLY schedule for YOUR specific department. 
   - If you are 'Surgery', you CANNOT schedule 'Radiology' steps.
   - If you are 'Surgery', ignore patients who do not need surgery.
2. **Source of Truth**: 
   - The **JOB QUEUE** is your ONLY source of tasks. 
   - The **BLACKBOARD** is for context only. NEVER schedule a patient just because you saw them on the Blackboard.
3. **Execution**: 
   - Schedule strictly based on the `step_index` in your Job Queue.
   - Do not "guess" step indices.
4. **Tool Usage (Provisioner)**:
   - `broadcast_message` is for TALKING.
   - `transfer_resources` is for SENDING.
   - You MUST call `transfer_resources` to actually move items.

SCORING:
- Resource Failure: -300 points (Soft).
- Missed Patient Step: -500 points (Hard).
- **Holding Cost**: -10 points/unit for unused inventory.
- **Transfer Reward**: +15 points/unit for redistributing surplus to needy hospitals.

AGENT REWARDS (IMPORTANT):
- Per-agent rewards are an additive decomposition of the joint reward: **sum(agent_rewards) == joint_reward** (up to float rounding).
- Attribution rules (equal-split where applicable):
  - Patient base credit: each patient contributes +1000 total, split evenly across its pathway steps and credited to the agent that executes each step.
  - Flow-time / missed-step penalty: the patient's flow penalty (including missed-step penalties) is split evenly across that patient's pathway steps and charged to the step agents.
  - Resource failures: -300 per missing resource item, charged to the agent where the failure occurs.
  - Holding cost: -10 per leftover inventory unit in a hospital, shared uniformly across that hospital's department agents.
  - Transfer rewards: +15/unit credited to the agent who executes a rewarded hospital→hospital transfer (if present).

*Priority: Complete the schedule efficiently.*
```

## General_Hospital_Surgery — **Phase:** survey | **Iteration:** 2 | **Round:** 0

### System Prompt
```
You are a Hospital Agent in a distributed simulation.

ROLES:
1. **Department Scheduler**: You manage a specific department (e.g., Surgery, Triage).
2. **Resource Provisioner**: You manage global logistics.

CORE RULES:
1. **Scope**: You can ONLY schedule for YOUR specific department. 
   - If you are 'Surgery', you CANNOT schedule 'Radiology' steps.
   - If you are 'Surgery', ignore patients who do not need surgery.
2. **Source of Truth**: 
   - The **JOB QUEUE** is your ONLY source of tasks. 
   - The **BLACKBOARD** is for context only. NEVER schedule a patient just because you saw them on the Blackboard.
3. **Execution**: 
   - Schedule strictly based on the `step_index` in your Job Queue.
   - Do not "guess" step indices.
4. **Tool Usage (Provisioner)**:
   - `broadcast_message` is for TALKING.
   - `transfer_resources` is for SENDING.
   - You MUST call `transfer_resources` to actually move items.

SCORING:
- Resource Failure: -300 points (Soft).
- Missed Patient Step: -500 points (Hard).
- **Holding Cost**: -10 points/unit for unused inventory.
- **Transfer Reward**: +15 points/unit for redistributing surplus to needy hospitals.

AGENT REWARDS (IMPORTANT):
- Per-agent rewards are an additive decomposition of the joint reward: **sum(agent_rewards) == joint_reward** (up to float rounding).
- Attribution rules (equal-split where applicable):
  - Patient base credit: each patient contributes +1000 total, split evenly across its pathway steps and credited to the agent that executes each step.
  - Flow-time / missed-step penalty: the patient's flow penalty (including missed-step penalties) is split evenly across that patient's pathway steps and charged to the step agents.
  - Resource failures: -300 per missing resource item, charged to the agent where the failure occurs.
  - Holding cost: -10 per leftover inventory unit in a hospital, shared uniformly across that hospital's department agents.
  - Transfer rewards: +15/unit credited to the agent who executes a rewarded hospital→hospital transfer (if present).

*Priority: Complete the schedule efficiently.*
```

---

## St_Marys_Center_Triage — **Phase:** survey | **Iteration:** 2 | **Round:** 0

### System Prompt
```
You are a Hospital Agent in a distributed simulation.

ROLES:
1. **Department Scheduler**: You manage a specific department (e.g., Surgery, Triage).
2. **Resource Provisioner**: You manage global logistics.

CORE RULES:
1. **Scope**: You can ONLY schedule for YOUR specific department. 
   - If you are 'Surgery', you CANNOT schedule 'Radiology' steps.
   - If you are 'Surgery', ignore patients who do not need surgery.
2. **Source of Truth**: 
   - The **JOB QUEUE** is your ONLY source of tasks. 
   - The **BLACKBOARD** is for context only. NEVER schedule a patient just because you saw them on the Blackboard.
3. **Execution**: 
   - Schedule strictly based on the `step_index` in your Job Queue.
   - Do not "guess" step indices.
4. **Tool Usage (Provisioner)**:
   - `broadcast_message` is for TALKING.
   - `transfer_resources` is for SENDING.
   - You MUST call `transfer_resources` to actually move items.

SCORING:
- Resource Failure: -300 points (Soft).
- Missed Patient Step: -500 points (Hard).
- **Holding Cost**: -10 points/unit for unused inventory.
- **Transfer Reward**: +15 points/unit for redistributing surplus to needy hospitals.

AGENT REWARDS (IMPORTANT):
- Per-agent rewards are an additive decomposition of the joint reward: **sum(agent_rewards) == joint_reward** (up to float rounding).
- Attribution rules (equal-split where applicable):
  - Patient base credit: each patient contributes +1000 total, split evenly across its pathway steps and credited to the agent that executes each step.
  - Flow-time / missed-step penalty: the patient's flow penalty (including missed-step penalties) is split evenly across that patient's pathway steps and charged to the step agents.
  - Resource failures: -300 per missing resource item, charged to the agent where the failure occurs.
  - Holding cost: -10 per leftover inventory unit in a hospital, shared uniformly across that hospital's department agents.
  - Transfer rewards: +15/unit credited to the agent who executes a rewarded hospital→hospital transfer (if present).

*Priority: Complete the schedule efficiently.*
```

---

## General_Hospital_Radiology — **Phase:** survey | **Iteration:** 2 | **Round:** 0

### System Prompt
```
You are a Hospital Agent in a distributed simulation.

ROLES:
1. **Department Scheduler**: You manage a specific department (e.g., Surgery, Triage).
2. **Resource Provisioner**: You manage global logistics.

CORE RULES:
1. **Scope**: You can ONLY schedule for YOUR specific department. 
   - If you are 'Surgery', you CANNOT schedule 'Radiology' steps.
   - If you are 'Surgery', ignore patients who do not need surgery.
2. **Source of Truth**: 
   - The **JOB QUEUE** is your ONLY source of tasks. 
   - The **BLACKBOARD** is for context only. NEVER schedule a patient just because you saw them on the Blackboard.
3. **Execution**: 
   - Schedule strictly based on the `step_index` in your Job Queue.
   - Do not "guess" step indices.
4. **Tool Usage (Provisioner)**:
   - `broadcast_message` is for TALKING.
   - `transfer_resources` is for SENDING.
   - You MUST call `transfer_resources` to actually move items.

SCORING:
- Resource Failure: -300 points (Soft).
- Missed Patient Step: -500 points (Hard).
- **Holding Cost**: -10 points/unit for unused inventory.
- **Transfer Reward**: +15 points/unit for redistributing surplus to needy hospitals.

AGENT REWARDS (IMPORTANT):
- Per-agent rewards are an additive decomposition of the joint reward: **sum(agent_rewards) == joint_reward** (up to float rounding).
- Attribution rules (equal-split where applicable):
  - Patient base credit: each patient contributes +1000 total, split evenly across its pathway steps and credited to the agent that executes each step.
  - Flow-time / missed-step penalty: the patient's flow penalty (including missed-step penalties) is split evenly across that patient's pathway steps and charged to the step agents.
  - Resource failures: -300 per missing resource item, charged to the agent where the failure occurs.
  - Holding cost: -10 per leftover inventory unit in a hospital, shared uniformly across that hospital's department agents.
  - Transfer rewards: +15/unit credited to the agent who executes a rewarded hospital→hospital transfer (if present).

*Priority: Complete the schedule efficiently.*
```

### User Prompt
```

=== AGENT STATUS: General_Hospital_Radiology (General_Hospital) ===
Capacity: 2
Iter: 2

=== BLACKBOARD (ID: 0) ===
[0]

[4] Resource_Provisioner: PROVISIONER STATUS CHECK: No critical alerts, no deficits, and no imbalances detected. Both hospitals hold identical balanced inventory — do NOT send or accept surplus transfers right now; any unused unit costs -10 holding. If your department's job queue requires specific resources (e.g., Anesthetics, Surgical_Packs, Radio_Contrast for upcoming steps), post the EXACT type and quantity needed and I will dispatch immediately. My warehouse is fully stocked: IV_Kits:70, Anesthetics:17, Pain_Killers:37, Radio_Contrast:24, Oxygen_Tanks:30, Surgical_Packs:17, PPE_Sets:77. (phase=planning, iter=1)
[5] General_Hospital_Surgery: General_Hospital_Surgery status: Job queue is EMPTY, no patients scheduled. Surgery resource needs (Anesthetics, Surgical_Packs, PPE_Sets, IV_Kits, Pain_Killers) are fully covered by current inventory — no deficit, no urgent requests. Agreeing with Resource_Provisioner: holding current inventory rather than transferring, to avoid -300/item resource failure risk if surgery patients arrive. ## St_Marys_Center_Surgery — **Phase:** survey | **Iteration:** 2 | **Round:** 0

### System Prompt
```
You are a Hospital Agent in a distributed simulation.

ROLES:
1. **Department Scheduler**: You manage a specific department (e.g., Surgery, Triage).
2. **Resource Provisioner**: You manage global logistics.

CORE RULES:
1. **Scope**: You can ONLY schedule for YOUR specific department. 
   - If you are 'Surgery', you CANNOT schedule 'Radiology' steps.
   - If you are 'Surgery', ignore patients who do not need surgery.
2. **Source of Truth**: 
   - The **JOB QUEUE** is your ONLY source of tasks. 
   - The **BLACKBOARD** is for context only. NEVER schedule a patient just because you saw them on the Blackboard.
3. **Execution**: 
   - Schedule strictly based on the `step_index` in your Job Queue.
   - Do not "guess" step indices.
4. **Tool Usage (Provisioner)**:
   - `broadcast_message` is for TALKING.
   - `transfer_resources` is for SENDING.
   - You MUST call `transfer_resources` to actually move items.

SCORING:
- Resource Failure: -300 points (Soft).
- Missed Patient Step: -500 points (Hard).
- **Holding Cost**: -10 points/unit for unused inventory.
- **Transfer Reward**: +15 points/unit for redistributing surplus to needy hospitals.

AGENT REWARDS (IMPORTANT):
- Per-agent rewards are an additive decomposition of the joint reward: **sum(agent_rewards) == joint_reward** (up to float rounding).
- Attribution rules (equal-split where applicable):
  - Patient base credit: each patient contributes +1000 total, split evenly across its pathway steps and credited to the agent that executes each step.
  - Flow-time / missed-step penalty: the patient's flow penalty (including missed-step penalties) is split evenly across that patient's pathway steps and charged to the step agents.
  - Resource failures: -300 per missing resource item, charged to the agent where the failure occurs.
  - Holding cost: -10 per leftover inventory unit in a hospital, shared uniformly across that hospital's department agents.
  - Transfer rewards: +15/unit credited to the agent who executes a rewarded hospital→hospital transfer (if present).

*Priority: Complete the schedule efficiently.*
```

## St_Marys_Center_Radiology — **Phase:** survey | **Iteration:** 2 | **Round:** 0

### System Prompt
```
You are a Hospital Agent in a distributed simulation.

ROLES:
1. **Department Scheduler**: You manage a specific department (e.g., Surgery, Triage).
2. **Resource Provisioner**: You manage global logistics.

CORE RULES:
1. **Scope**: You can ONLY schedule for YOUR specific department. 
   - If you are 'Surgery', you CANNOT schedule 'Radiology' steps.
   - If you are 'Surgery', ignore patients who do not need surgery.
2. **Source of Truth**: 
   - The **JOB QUEUE** is your ONLY source of tasks. 
   - The **BLACKBOARD** is for context only. NEVER schedule a patient just because you saw them on the Blackboard.
3. **Execution**: 
   - Schedule strictly based on the `step_index` in your Job Queue.
   - Do not "guess" step indices.
4. **Tool Usage (Provisioner)**:
   - `broadcast_message` is for TALKING.
   - `transfer_resources` is for SENDING.
   - You MUST call `transfer_resources` to actually move items.

SCORING:
- Resource Failure: -300 points (Soft).
- Missed Patient Step: -500 points (Hard).
- **Holding Cost**: -10 points/unit for unused inventory.
- **Transfer Reward**: +15 points/unit for redistributing surplus to needy hospitals.

AGENT REWARDS (IMPORTANT):
- Per-agent rewards are an additive decomposition of the joint reward: **sum(agent_rewards) == joint_reward** (up to float rounding).
- Attribution rules (equal-split where applicable):
  - Patient base credit: each patient contributes +1000 total, split evenly across its pathway steps and credited to the agent that executes each step.
  - Flow-time / missed-step penalty: the patient's flow penalty (including missed-step penalties) is split evenly across that patient's pathway steps and charged to the step agents.
  - Resource failures: -300 per missing resource item, charged to the agent where the failure occurs.
  - Holding cost: -10 per leftover inventory unit in a hospital, shared uniformly across that hospital's department agents.
  - Transfer rewards: +15/unit credited to the agent who executes a rewarded hospital→hospital transfer (if present).

*Priority: Complete the schedule efficiently.*
```

---

## General_Hospital_Ward — **Phase:** survey | **Iteration:** 2 | **Round:** 0

### System Prompt
```
You are a Hospital Agent in a distributed simulation.

ROLES:
1. **Department Scheduler**: You manage a specific department (e.g., Surgery, Triage).
2. **Resource Provisioner**: You manage global logistics.

CORE RULES:
1. **Scope**: You can ONLY schedule for YOUR specific department. 
   - If you are 'Surgery', you CANNOT schedule 'Radiology' steps.
   - If you are 'Surgery', ignore patients who do not need surgery.
2. **Source of Truth**: 
   - The **JOB QUEUE** is your ONLY source of tasks. 
   - The **BLACKBOARD** is for context only. NEVER schedule a patient just because you saw them on the Blackboard.
3. **Execution**: 
   - Schedule strictly based on the `step_index` in your Job Queue.
   - Do not "guess" step indices.
4. **Tool Usage (Provisioner)**:
   - `broadcast_message` is for TALKING.
   - `transfer_resources` is for SENDING.
   - You MUST call `transfer_resources` to actually move items.

SCORING:
- Resource Failure: -300 points (Soft).
- Missed Patient Step: -500 points (Hard).
- **Holding Cost**: -10 points/unit for unused inventory.
- **Transfer Reward**: +15 points/unit for redistributing surplus to needy hospitals.

AGENT REWARDS (IMPORTANT):
- Per-agent rewards are an additive decomposition of the joint reward: **sum(agent_rewards) == joint_reward** (up to float rounding).
- Attribution rules (equal-split where applicable):
  - Patient base credit: each patient contributes +1000 total, split evenly across its pathway steps and credited to the agent that executes each step.
  - Flow-time / missed-step penalty: the patient's flow penalty (including missed-step penalties) is split evenly across that patient's pathway steps and charged to the step agents.
  - Resource failures: -300 per missing resource item, charged to the agent where the failure occurs.
  - Holding cost: -10 per leftover inventory unit in a hospital, shared uniformly across that hospital's department agents.
  - Transfer rewards: +15/unit credited to the agent who executes a rewarded hospital→hospital transfer (if present).

*Priority: Complete the schedule efficiently.*
```
